Appendix H
Group theory and modular forms of
A_{4}

H.1
Group theoretical aspects

Here, we review the group theoretical aspects of
A_{4}
.

A_{4}
group is generated by two generators,
S
and
T
-transformations which satisfy the following algebraic relations:

\displaystyle S^{2}=(ST)^{3}=T^{3}=\mathbb{I}.

(H.1)

In
A_{4}
group, there are four irreducible representations, three singlets
\bm{1}
,
\bm{1}^{\prime}
and
\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}
and one triplet
\bm{3}
.
Each irreducible representation is given by

\displaystyle\bm{1}\quad\rho(S)=1,~\rho(T)=1,

(H.2)

\displaystyle\bm{1}^{\prime}\quad\rho(S)=1,~\rho(T)=\omega,

(H.3)

\displaystyle\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}\quad\rho(S)=1,~\rho(T)=\omega^{2},

(H.4)

\displaystyle\bm{3}\quad\rho(S)=\frac{1}{3}\begin{pmatrix}-1&2&2\\
2&-1&2\\
2&2&-1\\
\end{pmatrix},\quad\rho(T)=\begin{pmatrix}1&0&0\\
0&\omega&0\\
0&0&\omega^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

(H.5)

The Kronecker products between irreducible representations and the CG coefficients are shown in Table
H.1
.

The Kronecker products

the CG coefficients

\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}\otimes\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}=\bm{1}^{\prime}

a^{1}b^{1}

~~~~~~~~~~\bm{1}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{1}^{\prime}=\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}

(a^{1}b^{1})

\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}\otimes\bm{1}^{\prime}=\bm{1}

\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}\otimes\bm{3}=\bm{3}

(a^{1}b^{i})

\left(\begin{smallmatrix}a^{1}b^{3}\\
a^{1}b^{1}\\
a^{1}b^{2}\\
\end{smallmatrix}\right)

\bm{1}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{3}=\bm{3}

(a^{1}b^{i})

\left(\begin{smallmatrix}a^{1}b^{2}\\
a^{1}b^{3}\\
a^{1}b^{1}\\
\end{smallmatrix}\right)

\bm{3}\otimes\bm{3}=\bm{1}\oplus\bm{1}^{\prime\prime}\oplus\bm{1}^{\prime}\oplus\bm{3}\oplus\bm{3}

\begin{smallmatrix}(a^{1}b^{1}+a^{2}b^{3}+a^{3}b^{2})\end{smallmatrix}

\oplus\begin{smallmatrix}(a^{1}b^{2}+a^{2}b^{1}+a^{3}b^{3})\end{smallmatrix}

\oplus\begin{smallmatrix}(a^{1}b^{3}+a^{2}b^{2}+a^{3}b^{1})\end{smallmatrix}

(a^{i}b^{j})

\oplus\frac{1}{3}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}2a^{1}b^{1}-a^{2}b^{3}-a^{3}b^{2}\\
-a^{1}b^{2}-a^{2}b^{1}+2a^{3}b^{3}\\
-a^{1}b^{3}+2a^{2}b^{2}-a^{3}b^{1}\\
\end{smallmatrix}\right)

\oplus\frac{1}{2}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}a^{2}b^{3}-a^{3}b^{2}\\
a^{1}b^{2}-a^{2}b^{1}\\
-a^{1}b^{3}+a^{3}b^{1}\\
\end{smallmatrix}\right)

Table H.1:
The Kronecker products between irreducible representations of
A_{4}
and the CG coefficients.

H.2
Modular forms

Here we review the modular forms of
\Gamma_{3}\simeq A_{4}
.
The modular forms of level 3 of even weights can be constructed from the products of the Dedekind eta function
\eta(\tau)
and its derivative,

\displaystyle\eta(\tau)=q^{1/24}\prod_{n=1}^{\infty}(1-q^{n}),\quad q=e^{2\pi i\tau},

(H.6)

\displaystyle\eta^{\prime}(\tau)\equiv\frac{d}{d\tau}\eta(\tau).

(H.7)

Using
\eta
and
\eta^{\prime}
, the modular forms of weight 2 belonging to
A_{4}
triplet
\bm{3}
can be obtained as

[
12
]

\displaystyle Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}}(\tau)=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}\\
Y_{2}\\
Y_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix},

(H.8)

where

\displaystyle Y_{1}(\tau)=\frac{i}{2\pi}\left(\frac{\eta^{\prime}(\tau/3)}{\eta(\tau/3)}+\frac{\eta^{\prime}((\tau+1)/3)}{\eta((\tau+1)/3)}+\frac{\eta^{\prime}((\tau+2)/3)}{\eta((\tau+2)/3)}-\frac{27\eta^{\prime}(3\tau)}{\eta(3\tau)}\right),

(H.9)

\displaystyle Y_{2}(\tau)=\frac{-i}{\pi}\left(\frac{\eta^{\prime}(\tau/3)}{\eta(\tau/3)}+\omega^{2}\frac{\eta^{\prime}((\tau+1)/3)}{\eta((\tau+1)/3)}+\omega\frac{\eta^{\prime}((\tau+2)/3)}{\eta((\tau+2)/3)}\right),

(H.10)

\displaystyle Y_{3}(\tau)=\frac{-i}{\pi}\left(\frac{\eta^{\prime}(\tau/3)}{\eta(\tau/3)}+\omega\frac{\eta^{\prime}((\tau+1)/3)}{\eta((\tau+1)/3)}+\omega^{2}\frac{\eta^{\prime}((\tau+2)/3)}{\eta((\tau+2)/3)}\right).

(H.11)

Taking the products of these modular forms, higher weight modular forms can be constructed.
The modular forms of weight 4 are:

\displaystyle Y_{\bm{1}}^{(4)}(\tau)=Y^{2}_{1}+2Y_{2}Y_{3},\quad Y_{\bm{1^{\prime}}}^{(4)}(\tau)=Y^{2}_{3}+2Y_{1}Y_{2},

\displaystyle Y_{\bm{3}}^{(4)}(\tau)=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{2}_{1}-Y_{2}Y_{3}\\
Y^{2}_{3}-Y_{1}Y_{2}\\
Y^{2}_{2}-Y_{1}Y_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

(H.12)

In the same way, we can construct the modular forms of weight 6 as

\displaystyle Y^{(6)}_{\bm{1}}(\tau)=Y^{3}_{1}+Y^{3}_{2}+Y^{3}_{3}-3Y_{1}Y_{2}Y_{3},

\displaystyle Y_{\bm{3}}^{(6)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{1}+2Y_{2}Y_{3})\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}\\
Y_{2}\\
Y_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix},\quad Y_{\bm{3^{\prime}}}^{(6)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{3}+2Y_{1}Y_{2})\begin{pmatrix}Y_{3}\\
Y_{1}\\
Y_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

(H.13)

The modular forms of weight 8 are given by

\displaystyle Y_{\bm{1}}^{(8)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{1}+2Y_{2}Y_{3})^{2},\quad Y_{\bm{1^{\prime}}}^{(8)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{1}+2Y_{2}Y_{3})(Y^{2}_{3}+2Y_{1}Y_{2}),\quad Y_{\bm{1^{\prime\prime}}}^{(8)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{3}+2Y_{1}Y_{2})^{2},

\displaystyle Y_{\bm{3}}^{(8)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{1}+2Y_{2}Y_{3})\begin{pmatrix}Y^{2}_{1}-Y_{2}Y_{3}\\
Y^{2}_{3}-Y_{1}Y_{2}\\
Y^{2}_{2}-Y_{1}Y_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix},\quad Y_{\bm{3^{\prime}}}^{(8)}(\tau)=(Y^{2}_{3}+2Y_{1}Y_{2})\begin{pmatrix}Y^{2}_{2}-Y_{1}Y_{3}\\
Y^{2}_{1}-Y_{2}Y_{3}\\
Y^{2}_{3}-Y_{1}Y_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

(H.14)

Appendix I
Group theory and Siegel modular forms of
\widetilde{\Delta}(96)

I.1
Group theoretical aspects

Here, we review the group theoretical aspects of
\widetilde{\Delta}(96)
.

\widetilde{\Delta}(96)
group is generated by two generators,
S
and
T
-transformations which satisfy the following algebraic relations:

\displaystyle S^{2}=-i\mathbb{I},~(ST)^{3}=\mathbb{I},~T^{8}=(S^{-1}T^{-1}ST)^{3}=\mathbb{I},~S^{2}T=TS^{2}.

(I.1)

In Ref.
[
24
]
, it was shown that these algebraic relations correspond to
\widetilde{\Delta}(96)
.
In
\widetilde{\Delta}(96)
group, there are 40 irreducible representations as shown in Table
I.1
.

\end{matrix}

\begin{matrix}\bm{3}^{\prime}_{p}\otimes\bm{3}_{q}=\bm{\hat{3}}_{r}\oplus\bm{6}_{r+2}\\

\end{array}\right)\\

\begin{matrix}\bm{3}_{p}\otimes\bm{\hat{3}}_{q}=\bm{3}^{\prime}_{r}\oplus\bm{6}_{r+2}\\

\begin{matrix}\bm{6}_{p}\otimes\bm{\hat{3}}_{q}=\bm{3}_{r+2}\oplus\bm{3}_{r+6}\oplus\bm{\hat{3}}_{r}\oplus\bm{\hat{3}}_{r+4}\oplus\bm{6}_{r+2}\\

\begin{matrix}\bm{6}_{p}\otimes\bm{6}_{q}=\bm{1}_{r}\oplus\bm{1}_{r+4}\oplus\bm{2}_{r}\oplus\bm{2}_{r}\oplus\bm{3}_{r}\oplus\bm{3}_{r+4}\oplus\bm{3}^{\prime}_{r+2}\oplus\bm{3}^{\prime}_{r+6}\oplus\bm{\hat{3}}_{r+2}\oplus\bm{\hat{3}}_{r+6}\oplus\bm{6}_{r}\oplus\bm{6}_{r}\\
\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{1}\beta^{1}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{2}\beta^{6}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{3}\beta^{5}-\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{4}\beta^{4}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{5}\beta^{3}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{6}\beta^{2}\\
\end{array}\right)\\
\oplus\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{1}\beta^{4}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{2}\beta^{3}-\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{3}\beta^{2}-\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{4}\beta^{1}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{5}\beta^{6}-\frac{\sqrt{6}}{6}\alpha^{6}\beta^{5}\\
\end{array}\right)\\
\oplus P_{2}^{int(\frac{r}{4})}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{5}\beta^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{2}\\
-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}\alpha^{1}\beta^{4}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{2}\beta^{3}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{3}\beta^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}\alpha^{4}\beta^{1}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{5}\beta^{6}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{6}\beta^{5}\\
\end{array}\right)\\
\oplus P_{2}^{int(\frac{r}{4})}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}\alpha^{1}\beta^{1}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{2}\beta^{6}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{3}\beta^{5}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{3}\alpha^{4}\beta^{4}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{5}\beta^{3}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\alpha^{6}\beta^{2}\\

\end{array}\right)\\
\oplus\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{1}\beta^{1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{4}\beta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{6}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{5}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{3}\\
\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{5}\beta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{6}\\
\end{array}\right)\oplus\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{1}\beta^{4}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{4}\beta^{1}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{5}\beta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{6}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{5}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{3}\\
\end{array}\right)\\
\oplus\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{5}\beta^{5}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{1}\beta^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{1}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{4}\beta^{6}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{1}\beta^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{4}\beta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{1}\\
\end{array}\right)\oplus\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{2}\beta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{5}\beta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{1}\beta^{6}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{4}\beta^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{1}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{1}\beta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{3}\beta^{1}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{4}\beta^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{6}\beta^{4}\\
\end{array}\right)\\
\oplus\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{3}\beta^{6}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{6}\beta^{3}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{1}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{4}\beta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{5}\beta^{1}\\

\oplus P_{6}^{int(\frac{r}{4})}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{3}\beta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{6}\beta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{2}\beta^{1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{5}\beta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{1}\beta^{3}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{4}\beta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{3}\beta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{6}\beta^{5}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{2}\beta^{4}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{5}\beta^{1}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{1}\beta^{6}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{4}\beta^{3}\\

-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{3}\beta^{1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{6}\beta^{4}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{2}\beta^{3}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{5}\beta^{6}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{1}\beta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{4}\beta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{3}\beta^{4}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\alpha^{6}\beta^{1}\\
\end{array}\right)\\
\end{matrix}

I.2
Siegel modular forms

Here we review the Siegel modular forms of
\widetilde{\Delta}(96)
up to weight 5.
The Siegel modular forms of weight 1/2 are given by

\displaystyle Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}\zeta\\
\eta\\
\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}.

Taking the products of this, the Siegel modular forms of higher weights can be constructed.
The Siegel modular forms of weight 1 are:

Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}\zeta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\\
-\sqrt{2}\eta\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\\
\sqrt{2}\zeta\theta\\
-\sqrt{2}\zeta\eta\\
\end{pmatrix}
.

In the same way, we can construct the modular forms of weight 3/2 as

Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{1}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{1}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{\sqrt{6}}{2}\zeta\theta^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{6}}{2}\zeta\eta^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}\zeta^{3}\\
-\frac{3}{2}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{2}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\zeta\eta\theta\\
-\frac{1}{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\zeta^{2}\eta\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{2}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\\
\zeta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
.

The Siegel modular forms of weight 2 are:

Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{1}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\eta\theta^{3}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\eta^{3}\theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(2)}_{\bm{2}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{2}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\theta^{4}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\\
\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\eta\theta^{3}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\eta^{3}\theta-\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\\
2\zeta^{2}\eta\theta\\
-\zeta^{2}\theta^{2}-\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{4}\\
-\sqrt{2}\zeta^{3}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{3}-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta\\
\eta\theta^{3}-\eta^{3}\theta\\
\sqrt{2}\zeta^{3}\eta\\
\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
.

The Siegel modular forms of weight 5/2 are:

Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{2}\zeta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta^{4}\\
\zeta\eta\theta^{3}-\zeta\eta^{3}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}-2\zeta\eta\theta^{3}-2\zeta\eta^{3}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{4}\eta^{4}\theta-\zeta^{4}\eta\\
-\frac{3}{4}\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{5}-\zeta^{4}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\zeta^{5}\\
\frac{1}{4}\theta^{5}+\frac{5}{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{5}{4}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\frac{5}{4}\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{5}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2}\zeta\theta^{4}+3\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta^{4}\\
\frac{3}{4}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{5}-\zeta^{4}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{4}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{4}\eta^{4}\theta-\zeta^{4}\eta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta\theta^{4}+\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta^{4}\\
\zeta^{2}\theta^{3}-\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta\\
-\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{2}+\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(1)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta\\
-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{4}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\\
\frac{1}{4}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}
.

The Siegel modular forms of weight 3 are:

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{1}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{1}_{7}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{1}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{1}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\theta^{4}-3\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{3}_{2a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\zeta^{6}\\
-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta\theta^{5}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{3}-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta^{5}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{6}-\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{8}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{8}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{3}_{2b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{3}-\sqrt{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4}\eta\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{4}\eta^{5}\theta+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{8}\theta^{6}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{6}+\zeta^{4}\eta\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{3}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{8}\theta^{6}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{6}+\zeta^{4}\eta\theta\\
-\frac{1}{4}\eta\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{5}\theta+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{6}-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{3}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta\\
-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{6}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{5}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta\\
-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{5}\theta\\
-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{5}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\\
\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{5}\\
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(3)}_{\bm{6}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\\
-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{5}\\
-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
.

The Siegel modular forms of weight 7/2 are:

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{1}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{1}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{1}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}\zeta\eta\theta^{5}+\sqrt{2}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{2}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{2}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta\eta\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\theta^{6}-\frac{5}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{5}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{3}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{7}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4}\zeta\theta^{6}-\frac{3}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{3}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\\

\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\theta^{4}-\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{4}-\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\\
\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\theta^{5}-\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{1a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{3}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{7}\\
-\sqrt{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{3}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta\theta^{6}-\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\sqrt{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{1b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}2\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{3}+2\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta\\
\frac{1}{8}\theta^{7}-\frac{3}{8}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{3}{8}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{1}{8}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{8}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{3}{8}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{7}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\theta^{4}+3\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\\
-\frac{1}{8}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{8}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{3}{8}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\eta^{7}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\frac{1}{8}\theta^{7}-\frac{3}{8}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{3}{8}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{8}\eta^{6}\theta+\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\zeta^{5}\theta^{2}-\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{7}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\theta\\
-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\\
2\zeta^{5}\eta\theta\\
-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{7}\\
-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta\\

,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{5}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta\theta^{5}-\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\\
\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta\theta^{6}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(7/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3c}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(3/2)}_{\bm{6}_{3}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta\eta\theta^{5}+\frac{2\sqrt{6}}{3}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{3}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\\
\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{6}\theta\\
-\frac{2\sqrt{6}}{3}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{2\sqrt{6}}{3}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta\theta^{6}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{2\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\zeta^{6}\eta\\
\end{pmatrix}
.

The Siegel modular forms of weight 4 are:

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{1}_{0a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{1}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{6}}\theta^{8}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{2\sqrt{6}}\eta^{4}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{6}}\eta^{8}-\frac{4\sqrt{6}}{3}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{3}-\frac{4\sqrt{6}}{3}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{1}_{0b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{1}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{4}{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{8}{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{4}+\frac{4}{3}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{3}+\frac{4}{3}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\theta+\frac{1}{3}\zeta^{8}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{2}_{0a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{2}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{2}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{4}{3\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}}{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{4}+\frac{4}{3\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}-\frac{2}{3\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{3}-\frac{2}{3\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\theta-\frac{2}{3\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{8}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\eta\theta^{7}-\frac{7}{\sqrt{6}}\eta^{3}\theta^{5}-\frac{7}{\sqrt{6}}\eta^{5}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\eta^{7}\theta-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{4}-\frac{\sqrt{6}}{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{2}_{0b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{2}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{3}}\theta^{8}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{2\sqrt{3}}\eta^{4}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{3}}\eta^{8}+\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{3}+\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{3}\zeta^{4}\eta^{3}\theta\\
\zeta^{4}\theta^{4}+6\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}+\zeta^{4}\eta^{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{8}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta^{4}\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{8}\\
-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{5}-5\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\theta+\zeta^{6}\theta^{2}+\zeta^{6}\eta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{15}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{15}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}-2\zeta^{6}\eta\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{3}_{4a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{4}+\sqrt{2}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\theta^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}\\
\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{5}-2\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{3}+\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{3}_{4b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta\theta^{7}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{3}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{5}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\eta^{7}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{15}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{15}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}+2\zeta^{6}\eta\theta\\
-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{5}-5\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\theta-\zeta^{6}\theta^{2}-\zeta^{6}\eta^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{2a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{1}_{4}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{5}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{6}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{6}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{2b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\zeta^{6}\theta^{2}-\sqrt{2}\zeta^{6}\eta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{7}+\frac{9\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta\\
\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{9\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{7}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{2}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\theta^{6}-\frac{5}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{5}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}\\
-\frac{3}{8}\zeta\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{5}{8}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{8}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\zeta\eta^{7}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\frac{1}{8}\zeta\theta^{7}+\frac{1}{8}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{5}{8}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{8}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{6}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\theta^{5}-\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{4}+\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{6}_{0a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{2}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{8}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{8}\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{3}\theta^{5}+\frac{5}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{5}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta-\zeta^{7}\eta\\
\frac{1}{8}\zeta\theta^{7}+\frac{21}{8}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{35}{8}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{3}+\frac{7}{8}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\\
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta\theta^{7}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta^{3}\theta^{5}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\eta^{5}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{7}\theta\\
-\frac{5}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{5}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}+\zeta^{7}\theta\\
-\frac{7}{8}\zeta\eta\theta^{6}-\frac{35}{8}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{21}{8}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8}\zeta\eta^{7}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(4)}_{\bm{6}_{0b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{6}_{0}}Y^{(2)}_{\bm{3}_{0}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\theta^{8}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{8}\\
\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{3}\theta^{5}+5\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{5}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta\\
-3\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{2}-\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\\

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{3}_{6d}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3b}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}_{6}}

=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\theta^{8}-\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}-\frac{35\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta^{2}\eta^{4}\theta^{4}-\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{2}\eta^{8}\\
\frac{1}{32}\theta^{10}+\frac{13}{32}\eta^{2}\theta^{8}-\frac{7}{16}\eta^{4}\theta^{6}-\frac{7}{16}\eta^{6}\theta^{4}+\frac{13}{32}\eta^{8}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{32}\eta^{10}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{8}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{8}\eta^{2}\\
-\frac{3}{16}\eta\theta^{9}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{3}\theta^{7}+\frac{7}{8}\eta^{5}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{7}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{16}\eta^{9}\theta+\zeta^{8}\eta\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{0}}

=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2}}\theta^{10}-\frac{3}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{8}+\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\theta^{6}-\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\theta^{4}+\frac{3}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{8}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{10}\\
\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{7}+\frac{21\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{35\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}+\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{7}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\eta^{3}\\
-\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{6}-\frac{35\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{21\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{7}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\theta^{3}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{7}\eta^{2}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{4a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{4}\theta^{6}-\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{4}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{4}\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{6}-\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}+\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{7}\\
\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{3}\theta^{7}-\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}+\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{6}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{4b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3b}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{4}}

=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta\theta^{9}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{3}\theta^{7}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{7}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\eta^{9}\theta\\
\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{35\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}+\frac{21\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{7}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\theta^{3}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{7}\eta^{2}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{7}-\frac{21\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{35\sqrt{2}}{16}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{7}\eta\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}

=\begin{pmatrix}-\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{5}+\zeta^{4}\eta^{5}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{16}\zeta\theta^{9}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{7}-\frac{3}{8}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{16}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta+\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}\\
\frac{1}{16}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{8}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{7}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{16}\zeta\eta^{9}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}

=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2}}\theta^{10}-\frac{5}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{2}\theta^{8}+\frac{5}{8\sqrt{2}}\eta^{4}\theta^{6}-\frac{5}{8\sqrt{2}}\eta^{6}\theta^{4}+\frac{5}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{8}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{10}\\
\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}\\
-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0c}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3b}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}

=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{8}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{8}\eta^{2}\\
\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{9}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{7}+\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}-\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta^{3}-\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta\\
\frac{7}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}+\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{6}-\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{7}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{9}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0d}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3a}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3b}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{0}}

=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2}}\theta^{10}+\frac{27\sqrt{2}}{32}\eta^{2}\theta^{8}+\frac{21\sqrt{2}}{16}\eta^{4}\theta^{6}-\frac{21\sqrt{2}}{16}\eta^{6}\theta^{4}-\frac{27\sqrt{2}}{32}\eta^{8}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{16\sqrt{2}}\eta^{10}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{5}\theta\\
-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{7}-\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}-\frac{11\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{9}\theta\\
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}+\frac{11\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{6}+\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{7}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{9}\eta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4}}

=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{4}\theta^{6}-\frac{5}{4}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}+\frac{5}{4}\zeta^{4}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{4}\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{16}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{8}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{7}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{16}\zeta\eta^{9}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta\\
-\frac{1}{16}\zeta\theta^{9}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{7}-\frac{3}{8}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{16}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta-\frac{3}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3a}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3b}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{4}}

=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta\theta^{9}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{7}-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}\eta^{7}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\eta^{9}\theta+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\theta^{6}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{2}\theta^{4}-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{4}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{4}\eta^{6}\\
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}+\frac{11\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{6}+\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{7}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{9}\eta\\
-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{7}-\frac{15\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}-\frac{11\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta+\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{9}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{6}_{2a}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}^{\prime}_{3a}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{2}}

=\begin{pmatrix}-\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{7}-\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{5}+\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\theta^{3}+\zeta^{2}\eta^{7}\theta\\
\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{9}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{2}\theta^{7}-\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}+\frac{5}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}\\
-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{7}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\eta^{2}\theta\\
2\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}-2\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}\\
-\frac{5}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}+\frac{7\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{7}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{9}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{7}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}+\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{6}\theta-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\eta\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{7}\eta^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{6}_{2b}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{\hat{3}}_{7}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta\theta^{7}+\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{3}\theta^{5}-\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{5}\theta^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{7}\theta\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta\theta^{4}-\sqrt{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{3}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{5}\\
-\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{7}\\
-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\theta^{8}+\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{2}\theta^{6}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\eta^{6}\theta^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{2}\eta^{8}\\
-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\theta^{5}+\sqrt{2}\zeta^{5}\eta^{2}\theta^{3}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{5}\eta^{4}\theta\\
\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{7}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{5\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{4}\zeta^{3}\eta^{6}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
,

Y^{(5)}_{\bm{6}_{2c}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{6}_{1}}Y^{(5/2)}_{\bm{3}_{5}}\end{pmatrix}_{\bm{6}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{6}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{6}\eta^{4}\\
\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\theta^{9}-\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{4}\theta^{5}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{6}\theta^{3}-\frac{3}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{8}\theta\\
\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta\theta^{6}+\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{3}\theta^{4}-\frac{9\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta^{3}\eta^{5}\theta^{2}-\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{7}\\
-\zeta^{6}\eta\theta^{3}+\zeta^{6}\eta^{3}\theta\\
\frac{3}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta\theta^{8}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{3}\theta^{6}+\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{5}\theta^{4}-\frac{1}{8\sqrt{2}}\zeta\eta^{9}\\
\frac{1}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\theta^{7}+\frac{9\sqrt{2}}{8}\zeta^{3}\eta^{2}\theta^{5}-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{4}\theta^{3}-\frac{5}{4\sqrt{2}}\zeta^{3}\eta^{6}\theta\\
\end{pmatrix}
.
